        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
        if (!st.flags[ti] || !SAND_GATE.has(st.type[ti])) continue;   // cs:21208-21211
        const ai = st.idx(x, y - 1);
        if (st.flags[ai] || st.wall[ai] !== 0) continue;              // cs:21213-21216
        if (flag) {
          // ---- 中段：湖畔水盒棕榈 / 仙人掌（cs:21257-21279；水盒逻辑在

            && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 3)] === 255
            && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 4)] === 255) {

	private static bool PlantSeaOat(int x, int y)
	{
		if (Main.tile[x, y].wall > 0 || Main.tile[x, y].active() || Main.tile[x, y].liquid > 0 || !SolidTileAllowBottomSlope(x, y + 1) || !TileID.Sets.Conversion.Sand[Main.tile[x, y + 1].type])
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!SeaOatWaterCheck(x, y))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (Main.tile[x, y + 1] == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		TileColorCache cache = Main.tile[x, y + 1].BlockColorAndCoating();
		Main.tile[x, y].active(active: true);
		Main.tile[x, y].slope(0);
		Main.tile[x, y].halfBrick(halfBrick: false);

		return true;

/** GrowTree（L14171）：常规树。height=Next(5,17)；丛林草基座净空 +5 */
function growTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, y: number): boolean {
  let index1 = y;
  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
  // 头部适配检查：基座上一格三列无液体 / 基座活跃且适树且整砖(L29934:!halfBrick&&slope==0) /
  // 上格墙白名单 / 至少一侧邻格适树（侧根前提同款整砖检查）
  if (st.liquid[st.idx(i - 1, index1 - 1)] > 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i + 1, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !FIT.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  if (!TREE_WALLS.has(st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)])) return false;
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;
    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);
    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);
  };
  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
  const height = rng.int(5, 16);                       // Next(5,17)
  let clear = height + 4;                              // num3
  if (JUNGLE_GRASS.has(st.type[gi])) clear += 5;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - clear, index1 - 1)) return false;
  return growTrunk(st, rng, gs, i, index1, height);
}

/** GrowEpicTree（L11850）：巨型树。基座严格草(2)、邻格草族、净空 55、height=Next(20,30) */
function growEpicTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, y: number): boolean {
  let index1 = y;
  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
  const GRASS = idsByVanillaSheet(2);
  // 基座整砖检查(GrowEpicTree 同 GrowTree 头部语义:半砖/坡面拒绝)
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !GRASS.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
  const family = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 109);
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)
    && st.flags[st.idx(i + dx, index1)] && family.has(st.type[st.idx(i + dx, index1)]);
  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - 55, index1 - 1)) return false;
  return growTrunk(st, rng, gs, i, index1, rng.int(20, 29)); // Next(20,30)
}

/** GrowPalmTree（L11785）：沙滩棕榈，干身倾斜帧 fy=num4（±2 步进趋向 num3） */
function growPalmTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(i, y)) return false;
  let index1 = y;
  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
  // 基座整砖检查(GrowPalmTree:27280-27285 active && !halfBrick && slope==0)
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !PALM_GROUND.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i, i, index1 - 2, index1 - 1)) return false;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 1, i + 1, index1 - 30, index1 - 3)) return false;
  const height = rng.int(10, 20);                 // Next(10,21)
  const target = rng.int(-8, 8) * 2;              // Next(-8,9)*2
  let tilt = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < height; k++) {
    if (k === 0) {
      st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, PALM, 66, 0);
      continue;
    }
    if (k === height - 1) {
      st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(4, 6), tilt); // 顶段 fx=22*Next(4,7)
      continue;
    }
    if (tilt !== target) {
      const prog = k / height;                    // 倾斜步进阈值（L11814-11820）
      if (prog >= 0.25 && ((prog < 0.5 && rng.int(0, 12) === 0)
        || (prog < 0.7 && rng.int(0, 8) === 0)
        || (prog < 0.95 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) || true)) {
        tilt += Math.sign(target) * 2;
      }
    }
    st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(0, 2), tilt); // fx=22*Next(0,3)
  }
  return true;
}

/** 湖畔棕榈水盒段（CactusPalmTreesAndCoral 中段 flag 分支，cs:21257-21273）：
 *  沙面锚点 (x,y) 的 50×25 水盒（[x-50,x+50)×[y-25,y+25)，InWorld 内液量求和，
 *  常量 cactusWaterWidth/Height/Limit = 50/25/25，cs:4258-4262）内液量和 /255 > 25
 *  时掷 Next(4)==0 → GrowPalmTree（TryGrowingTreeByType(323) → cs:30508-30509）。
 *  返回 true = 水盒超限（Next(4) 已掷，可能已种棕榈）→ 仙人掌段不再 PlantCactus；
 *  返回 false = 水盒未超限 → 由调用方走 PlantCactus（cs:21274）。 */
export function waterBoxPalmCheck(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  let sum = 0;
  for (let k = x - 50; k < x + 50; k++) {
    for (let l = y - 25; l < y + 25; l++) {
      if (st.inBounds(k, l)) sum += st.liquid[st.idx(k, l)];
    }
  }
  if (Math.trunc(sum / 255) > 25) {
    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// ===================== GrowTreeWithSettings 族（宝石树 + 观赏树柳/樱） =====================
// ShimmerMakeBiome 的 aether 树走 TryGrowingTreeByType(583-589)（WorldGen.cs:30500-30542 →
// GrowTreeWithSettings 30546-30967）；AddTrees 的 1/20 观赏树走 616 柳 / 596 樱（cs:31558-31566）。
// 干身帧表与 GrowTree 完全同构（同一 switch，cs:30604-30700），复用 TRUNK。

/** GrowTreeSettings profile（cs:3795-3886）：宝石树七种 + 观赏树柳/樱共用同一骨架，
 *  差异只在 GroundTest/WallTest/SaplingTileType/TreeTileType（高度均 7-12、Padding 4） */

// 阶段 4 结构 pass:
// - FloatingIslands(pass 19,L5552 + CloudIsland L47397 简化):云岛(云 189 壳 + 土芯 + 草皮)
// - LifeCrystals(pass 55,L7828):area×2e-5 个生命水晶(岩层空腔地上)
// - HellHouses 已由 HellFortPass.runHellFortPass 取代
// - SurfaceDecor:生物群系感知地表(丛林/雪原树密度、沙漠仙人掌、丛林藤蔓/蘑菇)
import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
import type { GenState } from './GenState';
import { T, TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../../../data/tiles';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/items';
import { placeBuriedChest } from './BuriedChestsPass';
import { waterBoxPalmCheck } from './TreePass';
import { idsByVanillaSheet } from './TileRunner';
import { solidTile as _solidTileHB } from './HalfBrickPass';

const CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_189_cloud_block']!;
/** 雨云 196：CloudIsland/CloudLake 的雨云凸包（1/4）与岛上方 puff（1/2）专用；
 *  岛盘主体是云 189（cs:78724 num8）——196 误作主体会令土芯 type==189 判定恒假。
 *  云下雨幕由 WaterfallRenderer 画（WaterfallManager :204 type 11） */
const RAIN_CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_196_rain_cloud_block']!;
const EBONSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['v_112_ebonsand_block']!;
const CRIMSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['v_234_crimsand_block']!;
const JUNGLE_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!;
const CACTUS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_80_cactus']!;
const VINE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_52_vines']!;
const HEART = TILE_BY_KEY['crystal_heart']!;


// ================= Pass 23 FloatingIslands（cs:12976-13171 + CloudIsland cs:78618 + CloudLake cs:79068）1:1 =================
// 注册序 num=(int)(w*0.0008) 座岛 + skyLakes(4200 宽=1) 座天湖；
// 采样：x=Next(0.1w,0.9w) 避中央 ±150 → 与已放岛距 ≥180 → 该列自 y=200 起找首个活性格
// → y=Next(90, surf-100) 再 min(y, GenVars.worldSurfaceLow-50)；预算 w 次逐岛。
// 距离用 sqrt(dx²+dy²)（原版 Math.Sqrt——Math.hypot 有 ulp 级差异，禁用）
export function runFloatingIslandsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  const { w, h } = st;
  const isCount = Math.trunc(w * 0.0008);          // 3（4200 宽）
  const skyLakes = 1;                               // GenVars.skyLakes（w>8000/6000 才 +1/+1）
  const total = isCount + skyLakes;
  const housesX: number[] = [];
  const housesY: number[] = [];
  let lakes = 0;
  for (let n = 0; n < total; n++) {
    let budget = w;
    while (--budget > 0) {
      let ok = true;
      let x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);   // Next(0.1w, 0.9w)
      while (x > w / 2 - 150 && x < w / 2 + 150) x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
      for (let j = 0; j < housesX.length; j++) {
        if (x > housesX[j]! - 180 && x < housesX[j]! + 180) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      ok = false;
      let surf = 0;
      for (let y = 200; y < gs.worldSurface && y < h; y++) {
        if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { surf = y; ok = true; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      budget = -1;
      // GenVars.worldSurfaceLow（raw 截断）＝ gs.worldSurfaceLow（正数 floor≡trunc）
      const y = Math.min(rng.int(90, surf - 101), gs.worldSurfaceLow - 50);
      // GenVars.worldSurfaceLow（raw double 截断）——注意非 gs.worldSurfaceLow
      const isLake = lakes >= isCount;
          if (isLake) cloudLake(st, rng, x, y);
      else cloudIsland(st, rng, x, y);
          housesX.push(x); housesY.push(y);
      gs.floatingIslands.push({ x, y, style: 0, isLake });
      if (!isLake) lakes++;
    }
  }
}

/** WillWaterPlacedHereStayPut（cs:79050）：下/左/右三邻 = (活性实心非平台) 或 满水 */
function waterStaysPut(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const side = (ax: number, ay: number): boolean => {
    if (!st.inBounds(ax, ay)) return false;
    const k = st.idx(ax, ay);
    return (st.flags[k] !== 0 && !!TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.solid && !TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.platform)
      || st.liquid[k] === 255;
  };

function cloudIsland(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ci: number, cj: number): void {

    vx += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.05;

/** PlantCactus（cs:54176-54187）：锚点一次 GrowCactus + 150 次随机邻域尝试
 *  （x∈[i-1,i+1]、y∈[j-10,j+1]）→ 天然成簇 */
export function plantCactus(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, j: number): void {
  growCactus(st, rng, i, j);
  for (let k = 0; k < 150; k++) {
    const i2 = rng.int(i - 1, i + 1);        // Next(i-1, i+2)
    const j2 = rng.int(j - 10, j + 1);       // Next(j-10, j+2)
    growCactus(st, rng, i2, j2);
  }
}

/** RollRandomSeaShellStyle（cs:45176-45192）：Next(2) 起，1/10→2、1/10→3、1/50→4
 *  （四次掷骰顺序固定） */
function rollRandomSeaShellStyle(rng: RNG): number {
  let result = rng.int(0, 1);
  if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) result = 2;
  if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) result = 3;
  if (rng.int(0, 49) === 0) result = 4;
  return result;
}

/** PlaceTile 81 珊瑚（cs:60214-60218）生成期有效子集：上格空、下格整砖实心
 *  （cs:59455-59463）；frameX = 26*Next(6)。81 非固体 → 可在水下放置。 */
function placeCoral(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.inBounds(x, y - 1) || !st.inBounds(x, y + 1)) return false;
  if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)]) return false;
  const bi = st.idx(x, y + 1);
  if (!st.flags[bi] || !TILE_DEFS[st.type[bi]]?.solid
    || st.half[bi] !== 0 || st.slope[bi] !== 0) return false;
  st.setTileSilent(x, y, CORAL, 26 * rng.int(0, 5), 0);
  return true;
}

/** PlaceTile 324 贝壳 → Place1x1（cs:60166-60171 + 45194-45210）：下格 SolidTile2；
 *  frameX = 22*Next(3)、frameY = 22*style */
function placeSeaShell(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, style: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.inBounds(x, y + 1)) return false;
  const bi = st.idx(x, y + 1);
  if (!(st.flags[bi] && TILE_DEFS[st.type[bi]]?.solid
    && st.slope[bi] === 0 && st.half[bi] === 0)) return false;
  st.setTileSilent(x, y, SEASHELLS, 22 * rng.int(0, 2), 22 * style);
  return true;
}

```
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    this.slope[i] = s;
    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetSlope, x, y, v: s, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    this.type[i] = type;
    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
  }

  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
      this.wall[i] = wall;
      if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWall, x, y, v: wall, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
    }
  }

  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    this.wall[this.idx(x, y)] = wall;
  }

  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
  }

  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
  }

  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
    this.wire[i] = b;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

	public static void KillTile(int i, int j, bool fail = false, bool effectOnly = false, bool noItem = false)
	{

		{

		if (j >= 1 && Main.tile[i, j - 1] == null)

// 半砖是原版"水浸润到方块贴图透明区"与瀑布触发（WaterfallManager 靠 halfBrick 唇缘）
// 的核心：Smooth World 把地表凸起砸成半砖/坡面，Waterfalls pass 专门在水边唇缘砸半砖。
// 坡面渲染见 VanillaTiler.drawSlopedFrame（TileDrawing.cs:1328-1360 切条 1:1）。
// 仍近似原版处（逐条对照 1456 源码后保留）：

//    （黑名单/Boulders/生成期 190,30/ForbidsSloping 上方表均已 1:1，见 canPoundTile）
//  - SolidTile 的 tileSolidTop 近似为 !platform；PlaceTile 的 495 特判未移植
import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
import type { GenState } from './GenState';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../../../data/tiles';
import { idsByVanillaSheet, NOT_CLEARABLE } from './TileRunner';

/** 各排除表按原版 vanilla tile id 构建（经 idsByVanillaSheet 转内部 id） */
const T = { // 排除表用的原版 id 集
  SWITCH: idsByVanillaSheet(136),
  STATUE: idsByVanillaSheet(137),
  /** Loop B 主排除（16661）：137 雕像/48 尖刺/232 木刺/191 活木/151 沙岩砖/274 地牢砖? /75/76 黑曜石狱石砖 */
  LOOP_B_SKIP: idsByVanillaSheet(137, 48, 232, 191, 151, 274, 75, 76),
  /** 空位补角分支排除（16600/16602/16621）：151/274（不做补角材料）与 190/48/232（旁格排除） */
  NO_FILL: idsByVanillaSheet(151, 274),
  NO_FILL_SIDE: idsByVanillaSheet(190, 48, 232),
  /** Conversion.Sand（TileID.cs:30）：沙族 → Loop B 走 Tile.SmoothSlope */
  SAND: idsByVanillaSheet(53, 112, 116, 234),
  /** TileID.Sets.PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration（1456 TileID.cs:323）：48/137/232/191/151/274/135/442/428 */
  PREVENTS_SLOPES: idsByVanillaSheet(48, 137, 232, 191, 151, 274, 135, 442, 428),
};

/** blockType()==0 判定（悬顶分支旁格检查用）：非活性格或整砖格（half/slope 均为 0） */
function fullOrEmpty(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return true;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  return !st.flags[i] || (!st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0);
}

/** WorldGen.SolidTile(i, j, false) 等价（WorldGen.cs:42370）：
 *  active && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && !halfBrick && slope==0 —— 半砖/坡面格不算实心。
 *  这对 PoundTile（toggle 语义）至关重要：已砸的半砖不会被后续 pass 再"砸回"整砖 */
export function solidTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  // 原版 SolidTile 检查 active()（=flags）：TileRunner 幽灵 tile（有 type 无 flags）
  // 不得当实心——曾致补角分支实体化天空幽灵块成浮空半砖（2583 个）
  if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
  const t = st.type[i];
  if (t === 0) return false;
  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
}

/** 可砸判定（1456 WorldGen.cs:81434 CanPoundTile 1:1 表）：
 *  ① type 黑名单（cs:81448）：10 门/48 尖刺/137 陷阱/232 木刺/380 花盆箱/387 陷阱门/
 *    388 高闸/476 高尔夫洞/484 滚动仙人掌
 *  ② TileID.Sets.Boulders（TileID.cs:195）：138/484/664/665/711-716
 *  ③ 生成期追加：190 荧光菇块 / 30 木平台（cs:81462-81471）
 *  ④ 上格 ForbidsSloping（cs:81494）：21 箱/26 祭坛/77 熔炉/88/235 传送器/237 蜥蜴祭坛/
 *    441 假箱/467 箱2/468/470 模型/475 帽架/488 倒木/597 传送塔
 *  ⑤ CanKillTile 结构检查（cs:62249）——仍为近似（生成期主要拦截宝箱/巨石邻接，
 *    ①②④已覆盖生成期实际命中面）
 *  另要求 active 且 tileSolid 非 tileSolidTop（原版隐含：非实心块砸不出半砖） */
const POUND_BLACKLIST = idsByVanillaSheet(10, 48, 137, 232, 380, 387, 388, 476, 484);
const BOULDERS = idsByVanillaSheet(138, 484, 664, 665, 711, 712, 713, 714, 715, 716);

export function canPoundTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);

  if (BOULDERS.has(t)) return false;
  if (POUND_GEN_SKIP.has(t)) return false;
  // ④ 上格 ForbidsSloping（cs:81475-81478：上格 active 且禁坡物 → 不可砸）

const TREE_WALLS = new Set([0, 80, 106, 107, 138, 139, 140, 141, 145, 150, 152]);
/** 棕榈基座沙族（L11793：53/234/116/112） */
const PALM_GROUND = idsByVanillaSheet(53, 234, 116, 112);
const JUNGLE_GRASS = idsByVanillaSheet(60);
/** 植物（vanilla tile 20）：GrowTree 头部越过找基座（原版 while type==20） */
const PLANTS = idsByVanillaSheet(20);

/** 干身帧表：num5 case → num4(变体 0-2) → [fx,fy]（L14213-14425 逐 case 提取） */
const TRUNK: Record<number, Array<[number, number]>> = {
  0: [[0, 0], [0, 22], [0, 44]],
  1: [[0, 66], [0, 88], [0, 110]],
  2: [[22, 0], [22, 22], [22, 44]],
  3: [[44, 66], [44, 88], [44, 110]],
  4: [[22, 66], [22, 88], [22, 110]],
  5: [[88, 0], [88, 22], [88, 44]],
  6: [[66, 66], [66, 88], [66, 110]],
  7: [[110, 66], [110, 88], [110, 110]],
  8: [[0, 0], [0, 22], [0, 44]],
  9: [[0, 0], [0, 22], [0, 44]],
};

/** EmptyTileCheck（L15685，ignoreID=-1 简化：出界 false，任何实心 false） */
function emptyTileCheck(st: TileStore, x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number): boolean {
  if (x0 < 0 || x1 >= st.w || y0 < 0 || y1 >= st.h) return false;
  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

/** 树干+枝+根+冠 共体（GrowTree L14203-14606 / GrowEpicTree 同表）。
 *  groundType：基座 tile（判丛林加高）；返回是否成树 */
function growTrunk(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, index1: number, height: number): boolean {
  let flagL = false, flagR = false; // 侧枝去重（num5 5/7 左、6/7 右不同帧连续限制）
  for (let y = index1 - height; y < index1; y++) {
    // num4 = 枝型 0-9；顶/底强制 0；5/7 与 6/7 连续出现需重投
    let num5 = rng.int(0, 9);
    if (y === index1 - 1 || y === index1 - height) num5 = 0;
    while (((num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) && flagL) || ((num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) && flagR)) {
      num5 = rng.int(0, 9);
    }
    flagL = false; flagR = false;
    if (num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) flagL = true;
    if (num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) flagR = true;
    const num4 = rng.int(0, 2);
    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num5][num4];
    st.setTileSilent(i, y, TREE, fx, fy);
    // 侧枝帧（L14362-14426）：Next(3)<2 走 198+ 行（贴干枝），否则行内变体
    if (num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) {
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [44, 198 + k * 22] : [66, k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(i - 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);
    }
    if (num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) {
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(i + 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);
    }
  }
  // 基座方向 num7（L14439-14543）：两侧地面适配 → 决定根部与基座帧。
  //  侧根放置前提 = 邻格整砖(L30230/30234:!halfBrick && slope==0)——半砖/坡面侧不出根
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;
    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);
    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);
  };
  let num7 = rng.int(0, 2);
  const sideL = fitSide(-1), sideR = fitSide(1);
  if (!sideL) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 2; else if (num7 === 1) num7 = 3; }
  if (!sideR) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 1; else if (num7 === 2) num7 = 3; }
  if (sideL && !sideR) num7 = 2;
  if (sideR && !sideL) num7 = 1;
  const k8 = rng.int(0, 2);
  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 1) st.setTileSilent(i + 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 22, 132 + k8 * 22); // 右根
  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 2) st.setTileSilent(i - 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 44, 132 + k8 * 22); // 左根
  // 基座干身（switch num7，L14519-14566）
  const baseFx = num7 === 0 ? 88 : num7 === 1 ? 0 : num7 === 2 ? 66 : -1;
  if (baseFx >= 0) st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, TREE, baseFx, 132 + k8 * 22);
  // 树冠标记帧（L14568-14600）：12/13 fx=22，否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k
  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;

```

/** SolidTileAllowBottomSlope（cs:70211）：active && (tileSolid||tileSolidTop) &&
 *  非 topSlope(1/2) && !halfBrick。tileSolidTop≈platform（HalfBrickPass 同款近似） */
function solidAllowBottomSlope(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return true;            // cs:70214：出界按实心（InWorld 门）
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const t = st.type[i];
  if (!st.flags[i] || t === 0) return false;
  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
  return !!d && (d.solid || !!d.platform) && !(st.slope[i] === 1 || st.slope[i] === 2) && !st.half[i];
}

/** SeaOatWaterCheck（cs:58269-58313）：非海滩需邻域水量 ≥20（91×41 盒，非实心格液量和
 *  /255 ≥ 20）；海滩带（|x| ≤ beachDistance 侧）盒扩大到 131×50 且语义反转（水量少才放）。
 *  num4/num5（最近水距）算后不用，返回只依赖液量和 flag —— 已省 */
function seaOatWaterCheck(st: TileStore, gs: GenState, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  let num = 45, num2 = 20;
  let flag = false;
  if (x <= gs.beachDistance || x >= st.w - gs.beachDistance) { flag = true; num = 65; num2 += 5; }
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = x - num; i <= x + num; i++) {
    for (let j = y - num2; j <= y + num2; j++) {
      if (st.inBounds(i, j) && !_solidTileHB(st, i, j) && st.liquid[st.idx(i, j)] > 0) sum += st.liquid[st.idx(i, j)];
    }
  }
  if (Math.trunc(sum / 255) >= 20) return !flag;
  return flag;
}

  return true;
}

/** PlaceOasisPlant（cs:47543-47615）：Next(9) style 掷在海滩/边界早退门之前（每次调用必掷）；

function placeOasisPlant(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, X: number, Y: number): void {
  const style = rng.nextInt(9);                              // cs:47546——早退门之前
  if (X < gs.beachDistance || X > st.w - gs.beachDistance || Y < 5 || Y > st.h - 5) return;

    for (let j = Y - 1; j <= Y; j++) {                       // cs:47555：j < Y+1（两行）

  for (let i = X - 1; i <= X + 1; i++) {

    }
  }
}

i <= x + 45; i++) {\n    for (let j = y - 20; j <= y + 20; j++) {\n      if (st.inBounds(i, j) && !_solidTileHB(st, i, j)) sum += st.liquid[st.idx(i, j)];\n    }\n  }\n  return Math.trunc(sum / 255) >= 20;\n}\n\n/** PlaceOasisPlant（cs:47543-47615）：Next(9) style 掷在海滩/边界早退门之前——\n *  每次调用必掷（RNG 流关键）。门：3×2 盒内活性物须全为燕麦 529 且无液、\n *  盒下 3 格整砖沙族（Conversion.Sand）、OasisPlantWaterCheck 过 → 清盒后铺\n *  6 格 530（frameX=54*style+{0,18,36}，frameY={0,18}，style num2 恒 0 → num4=0）。 */\nfunction placeOasisPlant(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, X: number, Y: number): void {\n  const style = rng.nextInt(9); x < w; x++) for (let y = surface; y < h; y++) st.setTileSilent(x, y, SAND);\n  return st;\n}\nfunction count(st: TileStore, type: number): number {\n  let n = 0;\n  for (let k = 0; k < st.type.length; k++) if (st.type[k] === type && st.flags[k]) n++;\n  return n;\n}\n\ndescribe('绿洲消费段探针(临时)', () => {\n  it('登记绿洲后:棕榈 323 + 燕麦 529 + 绿洲植物 530 均出现且帧合法', () => {\n    const st = desert(2400, 500, 200);\n    const gs = newGenState(st.w, st.h);\n    gs.worldSurface = 260;\n    expect(placeOasis(st, new RNG(11), gs, 1200, 80)).toBe(true);\n    expect(gs.numOasis).toBe(1);\n    runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st, new RNG(1337), gs);\n    const palms = count(st, PALM), oats = count(st, SEAOAT), plants = count(st, OASIS_PLANT);\n    // 绿洲段逐格 1.0 概率尝试棕榈,环岸必有成树;530 需邻水(塘内液量 >> 5100)\n    expect(palms).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(plants).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    // 燕麦:海滩带(x≤380 或 x≥2020)干沙面必有种\n    expect(oats).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    // 帧公式:燕麦 frameX=18*Next(5) 或生长后 +90(上限 180);frameY=0\n    for (let k = 0; k < st.type.length; k++) {\n      if (st.type[k] !== SEAOAT || !st.flags[k]) continue;\n      expect([0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90, 108, 126, 144, 162, 180]).toContain(st.frameX[k]);\n      expect(st.frameY[k]).toBe(0);\n    }\n    // 530:6 格一组,frameX%18==0 且 <54*9,frameY∈{0,18}\n    for (let k = 0; k < st.type.length; k++) {\n      if (st.type[k] !== OASIS_PLANT || !st.flags[k]) continue;\n      expect(st.frameX[k] % 18).toBe(0);\n      expect(st.frameX[k]).toBeLessThan(54 * 9);\n      expect([0, 18]).toContain(st.frameY[k]);\n    }\n    // 棕榈落在绿洲 ±(width*1.5) 盒内\n    const num8 = Math.trunc(gs.oasisW[0]! * 1.5);\n    for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {\n      const k = st.idx(x, y);\n      if (st.type[k] !== PALM || !st.flags[k]) continue;\n      const inOasis = Math.abs(x - gs.oasisX[0]!) <= num8 + 2;\n      const inBeach = x <= 380 || x >= st.w - 380;\n      expect(inOasis || inBeach).toBe(true);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('无绿洲纯沙漠:尾部燕麦段在海滨干沙面生效(529>0),且同种子两次运行逐格一致', () => {\n    const run = () => {\n      const st = desert(1200, 300, 150);\n      const gs = newGenState(st.w, st.h);\n      gs.worldSurface = 160;\n      runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st, new RNG(42), gs);\n      return st;\n    };\n    const a = run(), b = run();\n    expect(count(a, SEAOAT)).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(a.flags).toEqual(b.flags);\n    expect(a.type).toEqual(b.type);\n    expect(a.frameX).toEqual(b.frameX);\n    expect(a.frameY).toEqual(b.frameY);\n  });\n\n  it('RNG 消费探针:placeOasisPlant 的 Next(9) 在早退门之前——y<5 也消耗(列均掷 yTop 次)', () => {\n    // 无水世界 + 无绿洲:非海滩列每列 = 1(列距骰) + yTop 次 Next(9)(燕麦水检先拒、零掷)\n    // 用计数代理核对总掷骰下界与结构\n    const st = desert(1200, 300, 150);\n    const gs = newGenState(st.w, st.h);\n    gs.worldDistance = 0 as never;\n    const calls: number[] = [];\n    const base = new RNG(7);\n    const proxy = new Proxy(base, {\n      get(t, p, r) {\n        const v = Reflect.get(t, p, r);\n        if (typeof v === 'function' && (p === 'nextInt' || p === 'int' || p === 'next')) {\n          return (...args: number[]) => {\n            calls.push(p as number);\n            return v.apply(t, args);\n          };\n        }\n        return v;\n      },\n    }) as RNG;\n    runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st, proxy, gs);\n    // 4 头掷 + 每列至少 1 列距骰 + 非命中列每 y 一掷(Next9) → 远大于列数\n    expect(calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(1200);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
 "replace_all": false,
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tests/_tmp_oasis_consume_probe.test.ts",
 "old_string": "    runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st, proxy, gs);\n    // 4 头掷 + 每列至少 1 列距骰 + 非命中列每 y 一掷(Next9) → 远大于列数\n    expect(calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(1200);\n  });\n});",
 "new_string": "    runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st, proxy, gs);\n    // 4 头掷 + 每列至少 1 列距骰 + 非命中列每 y 一掷(Next9) → 远大于列数\n    expect(calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(1200);\n  });\n\n  it('RNG 流恒等式:全空气+worldSurface=2 → total = 4头掷 + 1150列距骰 + Next(9)数', () => {\n    // yTop=1 → 命中列仙人掌分支(y 自 1)与燕麦段(y=0,下格空气必拒)均零掷,\n    // 非命中列恰好 1 次 Next(9)(placeOasisPlant 早退门之前)。列数 375+400+375=1150。\n    const st = new TileStore(1200, 300);               // 全空气\n    const gs = newGenState(st.w, st.h);\n    gs.worldSurface = 2;                               // yTop = 1\n    const calls: Array<[string, number]> = [];\n    const base = new RNG(7);\n    const proxy = new Proxy(base, {\n      get(t, p, r) {\n        const v = Reflect.get(t, p, r);\n        if (typeof v === 'function' && (p === 'nextInt' || p === 'int' || p === 'next')) {\n          return (...args: number[]) => {\n            calls.push([p as string, args[0] ?? -1]);\n            return v.apply(t, args);\n          };\n        }\n        return v;\n      },\n    }) as RNG;\n    runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st, proxy, gs);\n    const next9 = calls.filter(c => c[0] === 'nextInt' && c[1] === 9).length;\n    const colDice = calls.filter(c => c[0] === 'nextInt' && c[1] !== 9).length;\n    const headInt = calls.filter(c => c[0] === 'int').length;\n    expect(headInt).toBe(4);                           // 4 头掷(maxL/maxR/两死骰)\n    expect(colDice).toBe(1150);                        // 每列恰好一次列距骰\n    expect(calls.length).toBe(4 + 1150 + next9);       // 此外只有 Next(9),别无他掷\n  });\n});"
}

export function runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  if (gs.seedFlags.skyblock) return;
  const w = st.w;
  const num = 8;                             // cs:21120（remixWorldGen=2 不触发）
  const num7 = 380;                          // cs:21127
  const maxL = rng.int(3, 12);               // cs:21123 Next(3,13)——左段列距骰
  const maxR = rng.int(3, 12);               // cs:21124 Next(3,13)——右段列距骰
  rng.int(2, 5); rng.int(2, 5);              // cs:21125-21126 Next(2,6)×2——死骰（声明后未用）
  // ---- 头部绿洲消费段（cs:21129-21163）：每片绿洲 ±(width*1.5)×±oasisHeight(20) 逐格 ----
  for (let i = 0; i < gs.numOasis; i++) {
    const num8 = Math.trunc(gs.oasisW[i]! * 1.5);      // cs:21131 (int)(oasisWidth*1.5)
    const ox = gs.oasisX[i]!, oy = gs.oasisY[i]!;
    for (let j = ox - num8; j <= ox + num8; j++) {
      for (let k = oy - OASIS_HEIGHT; k <= oy + OASIS_HEIGHT; k++) {
        // cs:21138-21149：17×17 邻域已有棕榈 323 → 概率 1.0 降为 0.13（全扫不 break，
        // num9 赋值幂等）
        let num9 = 1.0;
        for (let l = j - 8; l <= j + 8; l++) {
          for (let m = k - 8; m <= k + 8; m++) {
            if (st.inBounds(l, m) && st.flags[st.idx(l, m)] && st.type[st.idx(l, m)] === PALM) num9 = 0.13;
          }
        }
        if (rng.next() < num9) growPalmTreeAt(st, rng, j, k);   // cs:21151-21152 NextDouble < num9
        if (plantSeaOat(st, rng, gs, j, k)) {                   // cs:21154
          if (rng.nextInt(2) === 0) growSeaOat(st, j, k);       // cs:21156
          if (rng.nextInt(2) === 0) growSeaOat(st, j, k);       // cs:21160
        }
        placeOasisPlant(st, rng, gs, j, k);                     // cs:21163（530，Next(9) 必掷）
      }
    }
  }
  const yTop = gs.worldSurface - 1;          // cs:21198：num13 = worldSurface - 1
  for (let n = 0; n < 3; n++) {
    let x0: number, x1: number, flag: boolean, maxValue: number;
    if (n === 0) { x0 = 5; x1 = num7; flag = false; maxValue = maxL; }
    else if (n === 1) { x0 = 400; x1 = w - 400; flag = true; maxValue = num; }
    else { x0 = w - num7; x1 = w - 5; flag = false; maxValue = maxR; }
    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
      if (rng.nextInt(maxValue) !== 0) {
        // ---- 尾部燕麦段（cs:21302-21320）：列距骰未命中的列，y 自 0 起整列铺燕麦/530 ----
        for (let y = 0; y < yTop && y < st.h; y++) {
          if (plantSeaOat(st, rng, gs, x, y)) {                // cs:21309
            if (rng.nextInt(2) === 0) growSeaOat(st, x, y);    // cs:21312
            if (rng.nextInt(2) === 0) growSeaOat(st, x, y);    // cs:21315
          }
          placeOasisPlant(st, rng, gs, x, y);                  // cs:21318（Next(9) 必掷）
        }
        continue;
      }
      // y=0 起扫（cs:21205）；y<1 时上格越界（C# OOB 读 = 空格 → 门必过但
      // 放置目标 y-1<0 不可写）——等价跳过
      for (let y = 1; y < yTop && y < st.h; y++) {
        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
        if (!st.flags[ti] || !SAND_GATE.has(st.type[ti])) continue;   // cs:21208-21211
        const ai = st.idx(x, y - 1);

1. 17×17 neighborhood scan: any active tile 323 → palm probability 0.13, else 1.0 (full scan, no break — num9 assignment idempotent, matches vanilla).
2. `NextDouble() < prob` → TryGrowingTreeByType(323) → GrowPalmTree (new local `growPalmTreeAt`).
3. PlantSeaOat → if true: two independent Next(2)==0 → GrowSeaOat (+90 frameX while <180).
4. PlaceOasisPlant(j, k, 530) — Next(9) style roll ALWAYS consumed (before the beach/bounds early-return gate).

Note: the task prompt's guess of "Next(7) palm count + Next(45,61)" was wrong — actual vanilla is per-tile NextDouble probability + PlantSeaOat + PlaceOasisPlant. (Next(45,61) is in PlaceOasis in the Oasis pass, already ported.)

**Section 2 — Tail sea-oat (cs:21302-21320)**:
For columns where `Next(maxValue) != 0` (previously a bare `continue`): y from 0 to yTop-1: PlantSeaOat → two Next(2) grows; PlaceOasisPlant(x, y, 530) per y (Next(9) each).

**New helpers (all in StructuresPass.ts, vanilla-cited)**:
- growPalmTreeAt (GrowPalmTree cs:27260-27352) — duplicated from TreePass.growPalmTree because it's private and I could only edit StructuresPass.ts; flagged for future dedupe by exporting from TreePass. NOTE: uses 1456-final clearance `num-20` (cs:27302) whereas TreePass still carries 1405's `index1-30` (1405:11800) — per project rule 1456 wins; flagged TreePass as a follow-up fix.
- palmEmptyCheck (EmptyTileCheck L15685 simplified, same as TreePass)
- solidAllowBottomSlope (cs:70211; OOB→true quirk preserved; tileSolidTop≈platform per HalfBrickPass convention)
- seaOatWaterCheck (cs:58269): 91×41 box (beach: 131×50), non-solid liquid sum/255≥20; beach zone inverts. Dead num4/num5 tracking omitted.
- plantSeaOat (cs:58315): gates = air/no-wall/no-liquid + SolidTileAllowBottomSlope below + Conversion.Sand {53,112,116,234} below + water check → place 529 frameX=18*Next(5), frameY=0. Color copy no-op at gen.
- growSeaOat (cs:58359): frameX<180 → +90. No RNG.
- oasisPlantWaterCheck (cs:47511): 45×41 non-solid liquid sum/255≥20; vanilla's quirk of testing parameter x (not loop var i) preserved as early-false.
- placeOasisPlant (cs:47543): Next(9) first (always consumed), then beach/bounds gate, 3×2 box must contain only 529/air and no liquid, 3 cells below must be SolidTile + sand family, water check → kill 3×2 (KillTile noItem during worldgen consumes zero RNG — verified 529 hits no genRand branch in KillTile) → place 6× tile 530 frames fx=54*style+{0,18,36}, fy={0,18}.

**RNG roll inventory (both sections)**:
Oasis head, per tile (j,k):
- 1× NextDouble (palm probability gate; always rolled)
- palm growth on success: Next(10,21) + Next(-8,9) + per-trunk-segment Next(4,7) top / Next(0,3) body + tilt Next(13)/Next(9)/Next(5) short-circuit sequence
- PlantSeaOat on success: 1× Next(5)
- if planted: 2× Next(2)
- PlaceOasisPlant: 1× Next(9) — always, even when the early gate rejects
Tail, per non-hit column: for y in [0, worldSurface-1): Next(5) if oat planted + 2× Next(2) if planted + 1× Next(9) (always).
